## Best date plans

### 2026-09-13 — Bell, Book and Candle + Sofreh

**Bell, Book and Candle, then dinner.** Persian at Sofreh. It is a 14-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 5:45 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=30+Lafayette+Ave&destination=75+Saint+Marks+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.bam.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/)

### 2026-09-08 — Winner on 7th + Starstruck

**Dinner, then Starstruck.** Bakery and cafe at Winner on 7th. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Starstruck starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=367+7th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=30+Lafayette+Ave&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.bam.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.winner.nyc/)

### 2026-09-09 — Gather Round: Let's Dance at Manhattan West — LayeRhythm + Superiority Burger

**Gather Round: Let's Dance at Manhattan West — LayeRhythm, then dinner.** Vegetarian diner at Superiority Burger. It is 2.0 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 5 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=385+9th+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=119+Avenue+A%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://manhattanwestnyc.com/) · [Restaurant](https://www.superiorityburger.com/)

### 2026-09-11 — Eyval + Marlon Magnée of La Femme with QUESTA

**Dinner, then Marlon Magnée of La Femme with QUESTA.** Iranian at Eyval. It is an 11-minute walk from the venue. Marlon Magnée of La Femme with QUESTA starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=25+Bogart+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11206&destination=599+Johnson+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11237&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.elsewhere.club/) · [Restaurant](https://www.eyvalnyc.com/)

### 2026-09-14 — Grand Morelos + Locust (Spectral+ Album Release Show)

**Dinner, then Locust (Spectral+ Album Release Show).** Mexican and Latin American at Grand Morelos. It is a 21-minute walk from the venue. Locust (Spectral+ Album Release Show) starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=727+Grand+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&destination=599+Johnson+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11237&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.elsewhere.club/) · [Restaurant](https://www.grandmorelosny.com)

### 2026-09-14 — Ruffian + Paramount+ Movie Nights: Galaxy Quest

**Dinner, then Paramount+ Movie Nights: Galaxy Quest.** Wine bar and chef's counter at Ruffian. It is 1.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Doors 5 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=125+East+7th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&destination=Sixth+Avenue+at+41st+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10018&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://bryantpark.org/activities/movie-nights) · [Restaurant](https://www.ruffiannyc.com/)

## Book ahead

#### [The Ford/Hill Project](https://www.bam.org/theater/2026/ford-hill-project)

2026-09-15 · 7:30 PM · theater · Tickets start at $45 · BAM Fisher, Fishman Space · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 60.0  
Waterwell stages verbatim Senate testimony by Anita Hill and Christine Blasey Ford in a four-person theater work created by Elizabeth Marvel and Lee Sunday Evans.
**Timing:** Runs Sept. 8-20, 2026; retained for the final book-ahead performances after Sept. 14.
**Practical note:** Post-show discussions follow every performance, with listed public conversations on Sept. 17, 19, and 20.
**Why now:** The run closes Sept. 20, leaving only a few performances after the near-term lane ends.
**Hot booking alert:** BAM lists the production as a short Sept. 8-20 run, so the final performances fall just beyond the seven-day window.
[Official](https://www.bam.org/theater/2026/ford-hill-project) · [Tickets / booking](https://commerce.bam.org/production/55728)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-09-20T19:30:00-04:00

#### [64th New York Film Festival tickets on sale](https://www.filmlinc.org/nyff/)

2026-09-15 · 12:00 PM · film · Passes $250-$700; single-ticket prices not listed · Film at Lincoln Center · Lincoln Square · Manhattan · Score 48.0  
NYFF64 returns to Film at Lincoln Center with its full festival slate from Sept. 25 through Oct. 12, 2026.
**Timing:** Festival runs Sept. 25-Oct. 12; NYFF64 tickets go on sale Sept. 15 at noon ET. Date re-verified against the venue: moved from 2026-09-25 to 2026-09-15.
**Practical note:** Use the schedule before the Sept. 15 public on-sale; some pass tiers are already sold out.
**Why now:** The public ticket on-sale is the first day after the seven-day window and is time-specific.
**Hot booking alert:** Film at Lincoln Center states NYFF64 tickets go on sale Tuesday, Sept. 15 at noon ET, with pass/member pre-sale access before the general public.
[Official](https://www.filmlinc.org/nyff/) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.filmlinc.org/nyff/tickets-and-passes/)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-09-15T12:00:00-04:00

#### [Limón Dance Company 80th Anniversary](https://www.joyce.org/performances/limon-dance-company-ns9f)

2026-10-06 · 7:30 PM · dance · Ticket prices not listed on source page · The Joyce Theater · Chelsea · Manhattan · Score 64.0  
The Limón Dance Company marks its 80th anniversary with José Limón's Mazurkas, live piano, and Aszure Barton's 2025 commission Join.
**Timing:** Runs Oct. 6-11, 2026; final listed performance is Oct. 11 at 2pm.
**Practical note:** One-week Joyce engagement with live music; the Oct. 7 performance includes a Curtain Chat.
**Why now:** The run lasts less than a week and closes Oct. 11.
**Book soon:** The Joyce lists the engagement as a limited Oct. 6-11 run, with a final Sunday matinee ticket link.

**Sale/deadline:** 2026-10-11T14:00:00-04:00

#### [The Imaginary Invalid](https://www.broadway.org/shows/the-imaginary-invalid)

2026-09-25 · 8:00 PM · theater · Ticket prices not listed on source page · Todd Haimes Theatre · Theater District · Manhattan · Score 62.0  
Bill Irwin returns to Broadway in his adaptation of Molière's comedy about Argan, a hypochondriac scheming to marry his daughter to a doctor.
**Timing:** First preview Sept. 25; opens Oct. 22; closes Nov. 22, 2026.
**Practical note:** This is a Broadway limited engagement with an official closing date, not an open-ended run.
**Why now:** The official Broadway listing gives a fixed limited-engagement closing date inside the book-ahead window.
**Book soon:** Broadway.org lists the production's closing date as Nov. 22, 2026.
[Official](https://www.broadway.org/shows/the-imaginary-invalid) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.broadway.org/shows/the-imaginary-invalid)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-11-22T20:00:00-05:00

2026-11-06 · 10:00 AM · art · Free · Luhring Augustine Chelsea · Chelsea · Manhattan · Score 57.0  
Luhring Augustine presents a solo exhibition of works by Jason Moran at its Chelsea gallery.
**Timing:** Opening reception Nov. 5, 6-8pm; exhibition on view Nov. 6-Dec. 19, 2026.

## Discovery coverage

_No API key is needed for these public websites. `not checked` is distinct from a checked source with no final pick._

- **[arts] 92NY — blocked (fetch failed):** Events fetch redirected to shared-session URL and returned HTTP 503.
- **[arts] Americas Society — not checked:** Optional source not reached before final assembly.
- **[arts] Anthology Film Archives — checked, no retained pick:** Fetched calendar exposed only header/minimal content and no retained screening.
- **[arts] Asia Society New York — not checked:** Optional source not reached before final assembly.
- **[arts] Atlantic Theater Company — blocked (fetch failed):** Optional whats-on URL returned HTTP 404.
